Analyst Stock Ratings
Overweight (O). The stock's total return is expected to exceed the average total return of the analyst's industry (or industry team's) coverage universe, on a risk-adjusted basis, over the next
12-18 months.
Equal-weight (E). The stock's total return is expected to be in line with the average total return of the analyst's industry (or industry team's) coverage universe, on a risk-adjusted basis, over

the next 12-18 months.
Not-Rated (NR). Currently the analyst does not have adequate conviction about the stock's total return relative to the average total return of the analyst's industry (or industry team's) coverage
universe, on a risk-adjusted basis, over the next 12-18 months.
Underweight (U). The stock's total return is expected to be below the average total return of the analyst's industry (or industry team's) coverage universe, on a risk-adjusted basis, over the next

12-18 months.
Unless otherwise specified, the time frame for price targets included in Morgan Stanley Research is 12 to 18 months.
Analyst Industry Views
Attractive (A): The analyst expects the performance of his or her industry coverage universe over the next 12-18 months to be attractive vs. the relevant broad market benchmark, as indicated
below.
In-Line (I): The analyst expects the performance of his or her industry coverage universe over the next 12-18 months to be in line with the relevant broad market benchmark, as indicated below.

Cautious (C): The analyst views the performance of his or her industry coverage universe over the next 12-18 months with caution vs. the relevant broad market benchmark, as indicated below.

Document 139: 261029_daiwa_ASE

Report source: line報告備份
Date: 2026-10-29
Pages: 7
Status: ok

### Page 1

Taiwan Information Technology

What’s new: ASE disclosed a strong set of quarterly results and guidance 
on 29 October, both beating market expectations, thanks to the strong AI 
scaling which is just getting stronger to benefit ASE’s leading-edge 
advanced packaging (LEAP) business. We raise our forecasts and 12M TP 
and reaffirm our Outperform (2) call on the stock; accumulate into any 
technical pullback.

What’s the impact: Results and guidance all beat. ASE reported 3Q26 
net profit of c.TWD27bn, beating our/consensus estimates by 25%/18% 
thanks to higher semiconductor assembly & test (SAT, or IC ATM by 
company definition) revenue which came in at TWD151bn (USD4.7bn), 7% 
above our estimate, for higher gross margin (GM) of 30.5%, 1.9pp above 
ours and topping ASE’s structural target of 24-30%. The strength was 
amplified further by lower non-operating losses, resulting in the strong 
bottom-line beat (table on p.2). Guidance was another beat thanks to the 
strong SAT business where ASE expects revenue to grow 11-13% QoQ for 
GM to top its record of 30.5% during the COVID upturn. We raise our 2026-
28E EPS by 14-24% assuming its SAT GM to reach 33% through 2027.

Strong AI gets stronger. Discussions centred on ASE’s LEAP business, 
where management further hiked revenue target this year beyond USD4bn 
(USD1.6bn in 2025), after 3 hikes YTD, thanks to strong demand for its on-
substrate (oS) packaging and initial ramp of its full-process solutions. With 
addition of new co-packaged optics (CPO) solutions, ASE expects the 
LEAP revenue to double YoY in 2027. As LEAP is margin-accretive, ASE 
expects the SAT GM to continue improving beyond 4Q26, making the 
ceiling of its structural GM target now as the floor to kick off 2027 for a 
strong start. Given management’s emphasis on “friendly pricing”, we expect 
likely continued cost passthrough across the supply chain to add to GM 
upside. Working hard to narrow the supply/demand gap, ASE further raised 
this year’s capex to USD11.5bn (from USD10.5bn), reinforcing our view 
that AI is scaling strongly to accelerate the chip industry growth.

What we recommend: Given our forecast revisions, we raise our 12M TP 
for ASE to TWD710 (previous: TWD550), based on our revised ROE-
adjusted PBR target of 5.8x (previous: 5.2x) 2027E BVPS due to a higher 
ROE trajectory. Our revised TP translates into 24x 4-quarter forward PER, 
which we see as justifying the rich PBR target given its 2026-28E EPS 
CAGR of 25%. We reiterate our Outperform (2) call with key downside risks 
of any “silicon inflation” and geopolitical overhang leading to AI capex cut.

How we differ: Our 2026-27E EPS are 5-10% above the consensus as we 
believe the street has yet to factor in its stronger LEAP business.

29 October 2026

Entering an even stronger earnings cycle (III)

➢ 3Q26 results and 4Q26 guidance all beat expectations 
➢ Strong AI scaling is getting even stronger to benefit LEAP 
➢ Reaffirm Outperform (2) call with a higher 12M TP of TWD710

Company profile 
Established in April 2018, ASE Industrial Holding Co (HoldCo) is a holding company which privatized 
ASE and SPIL fully on 30 April 2018 through equity and cash swap, respectively, and listed on the same 
day with senior management assigned mainly by ASE. HoldCo has become the strong No.1 OSAT 
maker in the world, offering a comprehensive product portfolio to its customers from wirebonding, flip-
chip & bumping, system-in-package (SiP) to broad-based electronics manufacturing services (EMS).

### Page 5

5

ASE Technology Holding (3711 TT): 29 October 2026 
ESG analysis

Update Date: 5 Feb 2026 
Source: Daiwa, Company

ESG risks
Risks Management Analyst comments
G
Executive/board
quality 2
ASE’s Board of Directors consists of nine members with diverse professional backgrounds.
Three of the directors are independent, representing 33 percent of the board. The Chairman also
serves as the Chief Executive Officer, resulting in unified leadership and centralized decision-
making. To maintain effective governance and oversight under this structure, ASE relies on its
independent directors and established board processes. The board currently includes one
female director, and the company continues to pursue long-term goals related to board diversity
and balanced representation. ASE has an Audit Committee, Compensation Committee, Risk
Management Committee, and Chief Government Officer to conduct effective supervision.
Capital management 3
In the past 5 years, ASE paid over 50% or nearly 50% of its earnings in dividends. However, the
dividend trend was volatile in dollar and payout ratio terms. We forecast its dividend payout ratio
to average over 50% in the next 2 years.
Related party &
transaction 2
ASE undertakes related-party transactions with its subsidiaries on sales and purchases. The
total related-party transaction sales/purchase amounts were less than 10% of its total
sales/purchases, and the transaction terms with related parties were normal compared with
those with other customers/suppliers. Thus, we do not think related-party transactions have a
meaningful impact on overall operations.
S Supply chainmanagement 2
The supply of raw materials has the most direct impact on ASE's operations and manufacturing.
To lower overall supply-chain risks, ASE has expanded the scope of sustainability risk
management to non-tier-1 suppliers. ASE currently has over 600 non-tier-1 suppliers.
ASE requires its suppliers to comply with its Supplier Code of Conduct, which aims to ensure
that suppliers conduct business in a manner that meets labour, health & safety, environment,
business ethics & management, and various corporate compliance standards.
E Water & wastewatermanagement 2
ASE has adopted 3 water-use strategies of reduce, reuse and recycle. Through its water
management measures, it reduced water consumption density (water consumption/revenue) by
43% in 2024 vs. that of 2015. ASE is committed to increasing water reuse at its facilities in order
to improve water efficiency.
E
Waste & hazardous
materials
management
1
ASE is committed to improving waste recycling and reuse through the 5 key elements of a
circular economy of: direct recycling, reuse, off-site regeneration, renewable materials, and
supply and rental. The recycling rate of general waste was 90% in 2024 and its hazardous waste
density declined by 53% in 2024 vs. that of 2015.
E GHG emissions 1
ASE is committed to achieving net zero emissions by advancing low carbon energy adoption,
developing smart grid technologies, and strengthening high tech energy management systems.
As of 2024, 19% of total manufacturing energy consumption comes from renewable sources,
88% of ASE facilities use renewable energy, and 10 sites have achieved RE100 certification.
Note: Management score represents a company's ability to manage/benefit from certain ESG topics. The scores range from 1 to 3, with 1 being the strongest.

ASE Technology Holding (3711 TT): 29 October 2026

Date: 2026-11-03

### Page 1

Taiwan Information Technology

What’s new: We attended Fositek’s investor meeting following its 3Q26 
results announcement on 3 November. We believe the company will benefit 
from the AI server trend with a rising liquid-cooling penetration rate in the 
following years. With rising server revenue over 2026-28E given its share-
gain potential, we reaffirm our Buy (1) rating. See our previous memo 3Q26 
revenue largely in line with expectations, 7 October 2026.

What’s the impact: 3Q26 results beat our estimates and Bloomberg 
consensus. Fositek’s 3Q26 revenue of TWD4,502m (+20.4% QoQ, 
+26.2% YoY), a record high, was in line with our estimate and the 
Bloomberg consensus. Its 3Q26 gross margin came in at 38.0% (vs. 34.0% 
in 2Q26 and 27.7% in 3Q25), also a record-high level, above our estimates 
(34.3%) and the consensus (36.5%), mainly on manufacturing process 
improvements and better-than-expected product mix (server revenue 
accounted for 81.6% in 3Q26, up from 54.9% in 2Q26 and 56.4% in 1Q26). 
Its operating margin of 32.4% (vs. 27.9% in 2Q26 and 22.2% in 3Q25), 
another record high, beat our estimate (28.9%) and the consensus 
(31.2%), on better opex control. Thus, its operating profit of TWD1,460m 
(+39.9% QoQ, +84.2% YoY) beat our estimate and the consensus by 
11.0% and 3.3%, respectively. Its net profit of TWD1,242m (+46.3% QoQ, 
+80.8% YoY) beat our estimate and the consensus by 15.9% and 6.5%, 
respectively. Its 3Q26 EPS finished at TWD18.11. Its 3Q26 product mix 
was: server (81.6%, +92.7% YoY), foldable phone (15.4%, -53.1% YoY), 
notebook (4.5%, -28.1% YoY) and others (0.6%, -10.8% YoY).

Brighter 2027 outlook, driven by server segment. Following the ramping 
up of GPU and ASICs projects in 2H26, we expect a stronger 2027 revenue 
outlook vs. 2026, along with gross margin expansion. With the rise in the 
liquid-cooling penetration rate, quick disconnect (QD) shipments per rack 
and QD spec upgrade for ASP expansion, we forecast its revenue to grow 
by 40.3% YoY in 2026 and 59.2% YoY in 2027. We raise our 2026-28E 
EPS by 9-29% on our more aggressive revenue and gross- and operating-
margin assumptions.

What we recommend: We reaffirm our Buy (1) call and lift our 12M TP to 
TWD2,910 (from TWD2,615), still based on a target PER of 32x (vs. its 
past-3-year range of 9-42x [average of 24x]), translating to a 0.6x PEG 
over 2026-28E on our 1-year forward EPS. Key downside risks: lower-than-
expected market share for key clients; slower-than-expected penetration 
rate of foldable phones; and geopolitical risks.

How we differ: Our 2028E EPS is 8% above the Bloomberg consensus 
likely as we are more aggressive on revenue.

3 November 2026

➢ Reaffirming our Buy (1) rating and raising 12M TP to TWD2,910

Company profile 
Fositek is a provider of foldable phone hinges, foldable laptop/tablet hinges, AR/VR glasses hinges, 
ultra-thin laptop hinges and keyboard hinges, as well as hinges for medical instruments and automobile 
applications. Its major clients include Huawei and Motorola.

### Page 5

Fositek Corp (6805 TT): 3 November 2026 
ESG analysis

Update Date: 7 May 2026 
Source: Daiwa, Company

ESG risks
Risks Management Analyst comments
G
Executive/board
quality 1
The CEO and chairperson of Fositek are not the same individual, and we see no issue with its
board quality. Besides, we note that the chairperson has 24 years of experience in this industry
and took over the role of chairman at Fositek in 2014, which is a positive sign from a governance
perspective.
Capital management 2 Fositek's dividend payout ratio was 40-60% over 2022-25, due likely to its capacity expansionplans.
Related party &
transaction 2 Revenue generated from related-party transactions (mainly for AVC and SZFSD) was 0-16% oftotal revenue during 2022-25, and we found no suspicious activity.
E Supply chainmanagement 1
Fositek maintains smooth communication channels with its suppliers and executes its business
operations effectively. In response to ESG issues, Fositek actively implements supply chain
management, striving to optimise the overall environment through product design, raw-material
procurement, manufacturing, transportation, packaging and recycling processes. It integrates
upstream and downstream suppliers to establish a green supply chain. For example, Fositek has
required its suppliers to sign environmental protection agreements since 2021, committing to
comply with hazardous substance control regulations.
G Data security 2
To protect company information and customer data, Fositek has passed the IEC/ISO 27001
information security management system certification and will continue to enhance execution in
various management areas.
Note: Management score represents a company's ability to manage/benefit from certain ESG topics. The scores range from 1 to 3, with 1 being the strongest.

Grand Process Technology Corp. (3131.TWO): SoIC / PLP to support 
future GM enhancement; 3Q26 GM miss
3 November 2026 | 10:54PM HKT
GPTC reported its 3Q26 preliminary results. Revenues was pre-announced, while GM 
came in at 36.6% (down 2.3ppt QoQ; down 4.2ppt YoY), below GSe/cons. of 
39.7%/40.6%, which we attribute to an unfavorable product mix. Opex ratio further 
improved to 14.6% (vs. 17.7% in 2Q26), better than our / Bloomberg consensus 
estimates of 15.1% / 16.3%, leading OP income to +34% QoQ, or +54% YoY. Overall, 
the miss on GM leads EPS miss our / Bloomberg consensus by 9% / 14%. Despite 
3Q26 GM miss, we believe GPTC’s multi-year growth outlook remains intact, with 
continuous capacity increase to better capture the demand from CoWoS capacity 
expansion, along with product mix upgrade toward SoIC and PLP , carrying higher 
content value. Maintain Buy.

Earnings revision: We factor in 3Q26 results and cut EPS by 4% / 2% / 0% in 
2026-28E, mainly on lower GM, as we slowdown the pace of product mix upgrade 
toward SoIC and PLP . Despite the cut, we continue to expect GM sequential 
enhancement, with 4Q26 GM up to 37.7% (vs. 36.6% in 3Q26) and up to 40.2% / 
42.5% in 2027-28E, driven by rising SoIC and PLP contribution.

Ratings, coverage universe and related definitions
Buy (B), Neutral (N), Sell (S) Analysts recommend stocks as Buys or Sells for inclusion on various regional Investment Lists. Being assigned a Buy or Sell
on an Investment List is determined by a stock’s total return potential relative to its coverage universe. Any stock not assigned as a Buy or a Sell on an
Investment List with an active rating (i.e., a  stock that is not Rating Suspended, Not Rated, Early-Stage Biotech, Coverage Suspended or Not Covered), is

deemed Neutral. Each region manages Regional Conviction Lists, which are selected from Buy rated stocks on the respective region’s Investment Lists
and represent investment recommendations focused on the size of the total return potential and/or the likelihood of the realization of the return across
their respective areas of coverage.  The addition or removal of stocks from such Conviction Lists are managed by the Investment Review Committee or
other designated committee in each respective region and do not represent a change in the analysts’ investment rating for such stocks.

Total return potential represents the upside or downside differential between the current share price and the price target, including all paid or
anticipated dividends, expected during the time horizon associated with the price target. Price targets are required for all covered stocks. The total
return potential, price target and associated time horizon are stated in each report adding or reiterating an Investment List membership.
Coverage Universe: A list of all stocks in each coverage universe is available by primary analyst, stock and coverage universe at

https://www.gs.com/research/hedge.html.

3481 群創光電(INNOLUX)

報告日期： 2026/10/27 研究員：曾智楷 (Kevin Tseng)  
壹、訪 談／法 說 內 容 
重點摘要： 
 2Q26 營收 637 億，毛利率 14.6% ，營利率 2.6% ，EPS 0.57
元。受惠於非顯示器比重持續提升 1H26 EPS 達 0.77 元。  
 公司今年處分三座廠房，累計獲利約為 200 億元，目前資產處
分已告一段落 ， 暫無進一步出售廠房計畫 ， 後續將配合FOPLP
投資調整既有廠區資源。  
 因上半年提前拉貨以及零組件漲價影響，消費型顯示器需求動
能相對保守；半導體事業部分， Chip-first 製程已進入量產，
每月出貨量約 4,000 萬片，而 RDL-first 與 TGV （玻璃基板）
製程部分，目前正與客戶緊密合作推進，預期玻璃基板封裝產
品將於 2028 年推出。

一、 2Q26 毛利率達 14.6% ，EPS 0.57 元 
群創 2Q26 營收 637 億，QoQ -4.6% ，YoY +13.1% ， 而獲利方面，
2Q26 營業毛利 92.7 億， QoQ -3.6% ，YoY +96.8% ，毛利率
14.6% ，QoQ +0.1ppts ，YoY +6.2ppts ，營業淨利 16.3 億，營利
率 2.6% ，2Q26 業外淨利 16.3 億，稅後淨利 46.5 億，單季稅後
EPS 0.57 元。而就上半年來看， 1H26 消費型顯示器佔整體營收
45% ，平均毛利率為 6%至 10% ；商用顯示器佔 13% ，平均毛利
率 16% 至 20%； 非顯示器領域佔42%， 平均毛利率11% 至 15% ，
受惠於商用、非顯示器比重提升， 1H26 EPS 達 0.77 元。

二、資產處分告一段落，後續聚焦 FOPLP 產能  
為充實營運資金、活化公司旗下資產，群創今年處分三座廠房， 3
月將南科模組廠以 8.8 億元售予南茂 (處分利益 6.59 億元 )，以及
將南科二廠以 63.25 億元售予矽品 (獲利約 58 億元 )。公司 4 月再
度宣布，將南科五廠房及附屬設施以總價 148.5 億元售予日月光
投控，預計處分利益高達 133 億元。累計今年群創認列的售廠總
獲利約為 197.59 億元。公司表示，目前資產處分已告一段落，暫
無進一步大規模出售廠房計畫 ， 後續將配合FOPLP 投資調整既有
廠區資源。

三. 消費性面板短期保守，持續朝高階封裝領域布局  
公司公告 9 月營收 199.95 億， MoM +6.9% ，YoY +0.7% ，3Q26
營收 575.8 億， QoQ -9.4% ，YoY -0.4% ，今年累計營收 1879.3
億， YoY +10.6% 。展望後市，在面板業務方面，受記憶體等零組
投資評等：區間操作 
目標價位：42.8~54.6 元 
     1.45x-1.85x27BVPS 
參考價位：46.75 元 
     1.58 X 2027 BVPS 
     2026/10/22 收盤

件漲價及筆電客戶提前於上半年拉貨影響，對消費性顯示器短期
動能相對保守，惟 50 吋以下 TV、高階電競與 OLED 顯示器，以
及商用顯示器支撐營運表現。而半導體事業部分，公司表示，目
前 Chip-first 製程已進入量產，每月出貨量突破 4,000 萬片，預計
今年與 2027 年將維持雙位數年增率 ， 至於RDL-first 與 TGV 製程
部分，由於 TGV 玻璃基板具備優異的散熱效率、低翹曲以及高密
度互連優勢，將瞄準 AI 與 HPC 晶片需求，目前正與全球策略夥
伴及客戶緊密合作推進 ， 預期玻璃基板封裝產品將於2028 年前推
出。預 估2026 全年營收 2451 億元 ，YoY +8.1% ， 毛利率14.2% ，
營利率 1.9%， 稅後淨利805 億 ， 全年稅後EPS 1.01 元。預 估2027
全年營收 2659 億元，YoY +8.5% ，毛利率 14.3% ，營利率 3.1% ，
稅後淨利 892 億，全年稅後 EPS 1.12 元，每股淨值 29.51 元。

四. 投資建議為 42.8~54.6 元區間操作  
公司持續投入半導體 FOPLP 領域，聚焦轉型、資產活化題材，但
考量到 4Q26 至明年上半年面板事業展望保守 ， 半導體事業部分，
後續仍須持續觀察客戶認證、量產進度及營運貢獻狀況。目前股
價交易於 2027 BVPS 約 1.64 倍 PBR 水準，以公司過往 PBR 介
於 0.4x~2.5x 區間水準，給予區間操作投資建議，目標價為
42.8~54.6 元(1.45x~1.85 x 2027 BVPS) 。

貳、產 業 及 營 運 概 況

一.公司簡介  
群創光電 (INNOLUX) 為全球第三大面板廠，主要產品為大尺寸 (大
於 9 吋)與中小尺寸 (9 吋以下 )與延伸技術之薄膜電晶體液晶顯示
器(TFT-LCD) 相關產品，大尺寸產品應用於液晶電視、公共看板、
桌上型監視器、筆記型電腦與工業控制等；中小尺寸則為平板電
腦、可攜式影音播放器、車載、航空船舶、智能家居及手機、穿
戴裝置等應用，也可選擇性搭配各類型觸控面板；另針對特殊使
用需求，也提供應用於醫療、軍用、教育與電子紙應用之產品。
集團總部位於苗栗縣竹南鎮，台灣生產基地位於竹南、南科、台
南、高雄等地，另有海外生產基地則包含中國深圳、中國寧波、
中國南京、中國南海、荷蘭等地。 2025 年公司營收占比大宗消費
品(Commodity) 55% ；非大宗消費品 (Non-commodity) 16% ；非
顯示器領域群 (Non-display) 29% 。公司 NB 客戶為 DELL，液晶電
視客戶包括 Panasonic 、Sharp 、Sony、Toshiba 、Samsung 、TCL
等；手機及平板電腦客戶為 Samsung 、Amazon 、Apple 及華為；
車用面板客戶包括 BMW、福 特、General Motors 、 特斯拉等車廠。
國內主要競爭廠商為友達光電、瀚宇彩晶，國外廠商則包括

2. 群創連售三廠房，預估獲利近 200 億元 
為充實營運資金並挹注未來發展動能，群創近期積極活化旗下資
產。繼 3 月先後將南科模組廠以 8.8 億元售予南茂 (處分利益 6.59
億元 )，以及將南科二廠以 63.25 億元售予矽品 (獲利約 58 億元 )
後，群 創 於15 日再度宣布 ， 將南科五廠房及附屬設施以總價148.5
億元售予日月光投控，預計處分利益高達 133 億元。累計今年以
來，預估公司可望認列售廠總獲利達 197.59 億元。

4 
二.產業趨勢概況  
1. AI 需求推動，FOPLP 與玻璃基板市場 2030 年將突破 80 億美元 
根據  Counterpoint Research 報告，受惠於 AI 與高效能運算
(HPC) 強勁需求 ， 全球扇出型面板級封裝(FOPLP) 與玻璃基板市場
規模，預計將從 2024 年的 6.5 億美元飆升至 2030 年的 81 億美
元以上，其中 AI 與 HPC 應用將佔 FOPLP 總營收的 45.6% 。相較
於傳統有機基板，玻璃基板具備高互連密度、尺寸穩定與抗翹曲
等優勢，能支援次世代小晶片 (Chiplet) 架構與大型 AI 處理器，吸
引台積電、 Intel 、三星電機、日月光及力成等大廠積極擴展產能
與研發。區域佈局上，預計 2030 年台灣、日本與中國大陸將囊括
全球 84.8% 的面板級封裝產能。儘管目前仍面臨面板尺寸標準化
與玻璃穿孔 (TGV) 製程等量產挑戰，但在產業鏈合作推動下，整體
市場前景依舊十分樂觀。

圖三、 2024-2030 年各地區 FOPLP 面板產能預估

資料來源： Counterpoint

2. 零組件成本壓力與需求衰退交織，10 月面板價格全面持平 
根據 TrendForce 預估，進入第 4 季後，受上游零組件成本上漲壓
力遽增及終端採購需求衰退雙重影響， 10 月 TV、MNT 與 NB 三
大面板價格預估將全面持平。 TV 面板部分，雖有原廠調控產能並
喊漲，但主要品牌第 4 季採購需求季減約 5%，短期難接受漲價；
而 MNT 面板需求持續減弱，惟成本壓力使面板廠無讓步空間，買
賣雙方對持平具共識，後續將視 TV 面板能否成功漲價而定；另
外， NB 面板則因前六大品牌第 4 季採購需求恐季減近 30% ，出
貨動能嚴重萎縮，在成本高漲且降價無助爭取訂單的情況下，雙
方亦達成維持價格穩定的共識。

營收走勢圖

研究員/莊佳蓉 EVA8894122@uni-psg.com

研究部對緯穎(6669)維持買進建議，主係：(1) 3Q26營收季增56%，
大幅優於預期；(2) 4Q26在AWS的ASIC氣冷出貨帶動下，預期4Q營
收季增個位數 ；(3) 雖T3水冷機櫃短期遞延， 但預估27年逐月攀升，
公司仍為主要供應商。(4) AMD MI450 於1H27年放量，客戶與平台
更加多元；(5) 新增之Oracle VR200預期的訂單約2.5K，約在27年
貢獻營收。 綜上所述，研究部上修 2026/2027 年營收至1.46兆元
/3.07兆元(原估1.27兆元/2.38兆元)，EPS上修至143.16元/241.16
元，目前評價僅8~9倍，位於低檔，給予12.5倍本益比，目標價3010
元(2027EPS*12.5PER)。

重點摘要： 
1. 3Q26營收大幅優於預期 
緯穎9月營收為1,726億元，月增19.6%，年增100%，整體3Q26
營收達4,346億元 ，季增56.2%，年增63%，營收達成率達到
135%(研究部預估)/128%(市場共識)， 大幅優於預期 ， 主係Meta
通用型及AWS T3氣冷出貨旺盛所致。3Q26財報尚未公布，研究
部上修至毛利率8.9%(原估8.1%)， 季減0.4pts， 優於原先預期 ，
主係通用伺服器占比較高， 產品組合較佳 ，對毛利率形成支撐，
預估3Q EPS約45.56元。

2. 4Q營運動能可望延續 
展望4Q26，雖水冷機櫃須遞延至年底至1H27才會逐漸放量 ，但
仍預期T3氣冷機櫃及通用持續出貨，預估4Q26營收約4,666億
元，季增7.4%，年增59.5%。毛利率8.2% (原估7.3%)，季減
0.7pts， 主係高單價ASIC水冷機櫃占比尚低， 預估EPS約45.18
元。整體而言，2H26在ASIC氣冷及通用伺服器帶動下，營運明
顯優於上半年

3. T3短期雜音不改長期趨勢，2027年水冷逐步放量 
根據供應鏈調查：(1) T3水冷機櫃初期產線磨合不順，客戶因
此下修今年出貨規劃。不過，2027年水冷機櫃出貨規劃仍逐季
攀升，在晶片端的水冷比重也提升 至50~70%，單價為氣冷3-4
倍以上，成為2027年營收成長的主要動能。在AWS下一代T4產
品，公司持續參與機櫃設計，有望採用全水冷設計，預期28年
出貨，為中長期成長動能。

### Page 2

請參見末頁重要批露及免責聲明  2

(2)MI450機櫃部分，Meta專案集中在2027年出貨，單價約450
～500萬美元，訂單逐漸明確，預估約可達5～7K櫃。(3) 新增
Oracle VR200訂單，預期約2.5K櫃，約在27年開始貢獻營收。
公司在GPU伺服器的客戶版圖進一步擴大。

4. 目前評價位於低檔，維持買進建議 
綜上所述，考量各客戶訂單逐漸明確， 研究部上修2026/2027
年營收至1.46兆元/3.07兆元(原估1.27兆元/2.38兆元)，2027
年毛利率雖然因高單價水冷機櫃及GPU機櫃比重提升，下滑至
6.5%(原估7.1%)， 但營收規模大幅擴張 ， 獲利仍可望顯著成長 。
股本部分，緯穎配發股票股利，除權基準日為9/8，配股率達
1.9828倍，股本由18.69億元增至56.02億元，EPS因此依比例
稀釋，故2026/2027年稀釋後EPS上修至143.16元/241.16元。
目前評價僅8~9倍，位於低檔，給予12倍本益比，目標價3010
元(2027EPS*12.5PER)。

附錄 一 ： 評 等 之 標 準 
評等  定義  
強力買進     預估未來 6 個月內的絕對報酬超過 25%以上  
買進  預估未來 6 個月內的絕對報酬 10% 以上  
中立  預估未來 6 個月內的絕對報酬介於 10～-10%  
降低持股  預估未來 6 個月內的絕對報酬介於 (-10%)以下  
未評等  沒有足夠的基本資料判斷該公司評等

### Page 1

현대차 (005380)

EARNINGS PREVIEW Company Analysis | 자동차/로봇 | 2026.10.16  
4분기 반등 기대

3Q26 Preview 
현대차는 3Q26E 매출액 42.4조원(-9.4% YoY, -13.9% QoQ), 영업이익 2.5조원(-
2.8% YoY, -13.5% QoQ, OPM 5.8%)을 기록, 컨센서스를 하회할 전망이다. 각 부문
별 영업이익은 자동차 1.7조원(-2.0% YoY, -14.9% QoQ, OPM 4.8%), 금융 5,501억
원(-4.5% YoY, -27.5% QoQ, OPM 6.9%)으로 예상한다. 
생산 정상화 및 신차 확대를 통한 수익성 개선 기대 
현대차는 글로벌 도매 판매 91.8만대(-11.5% 이하 YoY), 중국 제외 판매량은 90.9
만대(-9.6%)를 기록했다. 7~8월에 발생한 파업 영향으로 약 5.5만대 규모의 생산 차
질이 발생했다. 파업 영향으로 HEV 판매 증가율이 1%에 그치면서, HEV 판매 확대에 
따른 믹스 개선 효과도 시장 기대에 미치지 못했다.  
4분기부터는 3분기 파업 영향으로 지연된 생산량 회복 및 신차 확대가 본격화되면서, 
전분기 대비 매출 및 수익성 개선이 기대된다. 동사는 보급형 전기차 아이오닉3의 튀
르키예 생산을 9월부터 시작했으며, 이를 통해 유럽 시장 판매량은 점차 회복될 전망
이다. 또한, 1) HMGMA를 통한 HEV 미국 현지 생산 확대, 2) 제네시스 HEV 모델 순
차적 출시를 통해 내년부터 북미를 중심으로 믹스 개선이 본격화될 전망이다. 
투자의견은 Buy를 유지하고 목표주가는 520,000원으로 하향 
2027년 영업이익은 11.2조원(+10.2%)를 기록할 것으로 예상된다. 원화 약세 지속을 
반영해 이익 추정치를 하향하며, 이에 따라 목표주가를 하향한다. 다만, 현재 주가는 
12M Fwd P/E 기준 6.1배로 Legacy OEM 평균(7.7배)보다 낮은 수준이다. 1) RMAC 
성과 구체화, 2) RA(로봇 생산법인) 착공 등 Physical AI 사업이 구체화될 경우 밸류에
이션 할증이 가능할 전망이다. 
 Analyst 이병근 
bglee@ls-sec.co.kr 
Buy (유지)

### Page 1

한화에어로스페이스 (012450)

EARNINGS REVIEW Company Analysis | 방위산업 | 2026. 11. 2  
방산 납품 계획에 따른 일시적 실적 급감

방산 납품 계획에 따른 일시적 실적 급감 
지상방산 매출 1조 2,450억원으로 전년비 41%, 영업이익 217억원으로 전년비 
96.2% 급감하며 영업이익률은 1.7% 기록. 실적 급감에 대해 사측은 올해 주요 사업
의 납품 일정이 4분기에 집중됨에 따라 3분기 인도 물량이 적었던 것과 대전 사업장 
생산 중단에 따른 영향 때문이라고 설명. 단, 4분기에는 당초 계획에 따라 주요 사업의 
납품 물량이 집중되어 있어 실적이 개선될 것이고 대전 사업장은 내년 상반기 내에 정
상 가돌 하는 것이 목표라고 밝힘 
수주잔고 증가세 지속으로 성장 지속 가능할 것 
3분기 말 수주잔고 35조원으로 전년비 12.9% 증가. 이를 통한 지상방산 부문 성장 
지속 가능할 것으로 판단. 참고로 사측은 내년 매출 15% 수준 성장 전망 유지. 하지
만 금리 상승에 따른 글로벌 업종 평균 밸류에이션 하락에 따른 방산 부문 적용 멀티
플 하향 조정 그리고 상장 연결회사(한화오션, 한화시스템, 쎄트렉아이)와 상장 비연결
회사(한국항공우주)의 주가 하락 반영 영향으로 목표주가는 하향 조정 
8월 미 육군 차륜형 자주포 사업에서 시제 사업자로 선정. 향후 K9MH는 미 육군의 
작전 운용 개념에 맞춰 맞춤 조정을 거친 후에 특별한 결함 혹은 결격 사유가 없으면 
최종 계약 체결 예정. 동시에 미 육군의 궤도형 자주포 사업에도 참여할 계획. 뿐만 아
니라 스페인 K9, 크로아티아 천무 등으로 신시장 개척 역시 지속되고 있다는 점도 긍
정적 
 Analyst 이재광 
rheejaekwang @lssec.co.kr 
Buy (유지)

Document 299: AI编程之后下一个爆发点需等待---摩根大通双双下调智谱和MiniMax

Source PDF: all report pdf/AI编程之后下一个爆发点需等待---摩根大通双双下调智谱和MiniMax.pdf
Report source: WallstreetCN (Global)
Date: 2026-11-04
Pages: 5
Status: ok

### Page 1

摩根⼤通下调智谱⽬标价⾄ 1200 港元、维持增持， MiniMax 下调⾄ 200 港元、维持中性，
估值倍数由 20 倍压缩⾄ 12 倍 2030 年预期市盈率，主要反映下⼀波企业 AI 需求拐点晚于预
期。但摩根⼤通认为市场反应过度，当前市场处于编码与更⼴泛⽩领⼯作需求之间的 " 空档
期 " ，下⼀个需求台阶是时间问题。
摘要：
摩根⼤通下调智谱和 MiniMax ⽬标价，认为AI 编码需求之后的下⼀波企业需求拐点到来时间晚于
预期，但判断市场反应过度，仍维持对中国 AI 模型⾏业的整体看涨⽴场。
11 ⽉ 3 ⽇，摩根⼤通徐⽂韬团队将智谱⽬标价从 2,000 港元下调⾄ 1,200 港元，评级维持 " 增持 " ；
将 MiniMax ⽬标价从 300 港元下调⾄ 200 港元，评级维持 " 中性 " 。
两只股票的估值基准均由 20 倍 2030 年预期市盈率下调⾄ 12 倍，主要反映下⼀个需求场景拐点时
间的不确定性，不过智谱仍是摩根⼤通的⾸选股。
两只股票均已从 2026 年峰值⼤幅回调，智谱跌约 78% 、 MiniMax 跌约 85% 。

但摩根⼤通认为，token 使⽤量持续攀升、算⼒约束⽽⾮需求疲软制约中国市场、以及智谱⽑利
率持续改善等多重信号表明，当前市场对⾏业的悲观推断存在偏差。
编码之后的 " 空档期 " ：需求分步迈进
⾸⻚ 资讯 快讯 ⾏情 ⽇历 APP VIP 会员 ⼤师课 ⻅闻历 搜索

### Page 2

摩根⼤通将 AI 模型企业市场类⽐为劳动⼒市场：模型是劳动⼒，企业是雇主，模型公司则是兼营
⼈事业务的培训学校。
在这⼀框架下，企业对 AI 模型的需求是以 " 台阶式 " 跳升，只有当模型能够可靠完成⼀类新⼯作
时，企业才会⼤规模 " 录⽤ " ，此后任务量可能急剧增加。
据摩根⼤通，⽬前市场正处于编码与更⼴泛⽩领⼯作两个台阶之间的空档期。编码需求已验证
这⼀模式。
Cursor 年化收⼊从 2025 年 11 ⽉约 10 亿美元增⾄ 2026 年 6 ⽉逾 40 亿美元（据 Dealroom ），触发点
正是编码智能体变得可靠的时间节点。法律科技公司 Harvey 的 ARR 在不到两年内增⻓约 8 倍，显
⽰同样的路径正在其他垂直⾏业复制。
摩根⼤通指出，今天其他⽩领⼯作的状态，类似于 2024 年的编码⼯作，需求初露端倪但尚未爆
发。
独⽴测评机构 METR 的数据显⽰，当前最优模型在约 80% 的情况下能完成 3 ⼩时⼯作量的任务，
⽽模型可独⽴完成的任务时⻓⼤约每 105 天翻⼀番。摩根⼤通据此判断，下⼀个需求台阶 " 是时

间问题 " 。

### Page 3

与此同时，摩根⼤通强调算⼒供应约束是压制中国 AI 收⼊的主要因素。
商品化与定价权并存，价格下跌不等于利润率受损
市场另⼀⼤担忧是 token 价格持续下跌是否代表实验室丧失定价权。摩根⼤通认为两者可以并
存，并以智谱和美国市场数据加以佐证。
据 Ramp AI 指数，⾃ 2025 年 8 ⽉以来，美国样本中最⼤供应商的每百万 token 综合价格已减半，
但企业 token ⽀出增⾄约 15 倍，原因是 token 消耗量增⾄约 36 倍。
中国⽅⾯， 2024 年价格战令 API 标价下跌 90% 以上，此后⽇均 token 调⽤量增加逾千倍，从 2024
年初约 1,000 亿增⾄ 2026 年 6 ⽉超过 500 万亿。
摩根⼤通将模型分层定价的逻辑与学历市场类⽐：⽼⼀代模型等同于低学历，遭遇降薪（降价）
以换取使⽤量；⽽率先达到新能⼒标准的顶尖模型享有溢价窗⼝期。
过去两个⽉，中美七家头部实验室发布逾⼗款新模型，逾半数定价低于前代，但 OpenAI 的 GPT-
6 Astra 和⾕歌的 Gemini 4 Argon 两款顶尖新品均实现提价，摩根⼤通认为这印证了定价权依然

存在于前沿模型层⾯。

### Page 4

在⻓智能体任务中，质量的重要性尤为突出：单步准确率从 95% 提升⾄ 99% ， 20 步任务的成功
率可从 36% 跳升⾄ 82% ，使得顶尖模型的替代品⽆法仅凭低价取胜。智谱的 GLM-5.3 API 价格较
前代提⾼⼀倍，同时推出低价版 GLM-5.3-Flash 留住价格敏感⽤⼾，以 " 双杆并拉 " 的⽅式兼顾份
额与定价权。
智谱开放平台及 API 业务⽑利率在 2026 年上半年从⼀年前的 -0.4% 升⾄ 24.6% ，摩根⼤通认为随
着芯⽚、基础设施和模型硬件协同设计持续改善，⽑利率还有进⼀步提升空间。
智谱：前沿地位稳固， GLM-5.5/6 为近期催化剂
摩根⼤通将智谱定位为 " 中国少数仍在培养前沿毕业⽣的学校之⼀ " ，认为在需求价值持续向前沿
企业集聚的格局下，智谱具备持续捕获最⼤机会的条件。
智谱 ARR 增⻓轨迹印证了上述判断：从 2025 年 12 ⽉约 3,500 万美元增⾄ 2026 年 8 ⽉ 18 亿美元，约
8 个⽉内增⻓约 46 倍；管理层随后将年末指引从 24 亿美元上调⾄ 30 亿美元。公司 9 ⽉通过增发及

可转债筹集 50 亿美元，⽤于⽀持下⼀代模型训练和算⼒投⼊。
摩根⼤通将 2026 年收⼊预测从 61 亿元⼈⺠币下调⾄ 57 亿元⼈⺠币，同时将 2027 年收⼊预测从
178 亿元上调⾄ 239 亿元。⽬标价下调主要源于估值倍数压缩 ——新⽬标价基于 12 倍 2030 年预期
市盈率，剔除了此前给予公司相对于国内⼀线互联⽹企业的⼤部分溢价。
下⼀个近期观察点是预计 11 ⽉发布的 GLM-5.5/GLM-6 ，模型参数预计达 2.5 万亿⾄ 3.5 万亿。摩
根⼤通将关注新模型是否缩⼩与美国前沿模型的差距，以及企业是否开始将其⽤于更⻓的多步
骤智能体⼯作。
MiniMax ：性价⽐策略受认可，但前沿地位尚待证明
相较智谱，摩根⼤通对 MiniMax 态度审慎，评级维持 " 中性 " 。
MiniMax 今年主要在性价⽐层⾯竞争， M3 和 M3.1 Flash 均以领先实验室中最低区间的价格提供
有实⼒的模型，但公司全年未上调 ARR 指引，摩根⼤通认为这表明其性价⽐策略尚未转化为企

业需求的实质升级。
摩根⼤通同样将 2030 年预期市盈率倍数从 20 倍下调⾄ 12 倍，对应⽬标价从 300 港元下调⾄ 200 港
元。

### Page 5

⻛险提⽰及免责条款
市场有⻛险，投资需谨慎。本⽂不构成个⼈投资建议，也未考虑到个别⽤⼾特殊的投资⽬标、财务状况或需要。⽤⼾应考虑本⽂
中的任何意⻅、观点或结论是否符合其特定状况。据此投资，责任⾃负。

缩⼩与中美领先模型的差距，并按前沿模型定价，⽽⾮延续性价⽐路线。研报指出：
摩根⼤通列出的主要下⾏⻛险包括：追随者能⼒追赶速度加快导致溢价窗⼝期缩短、拥有云业

Document 305: Anthropic还在疯狂抢算力：8954亿美元承诺，连旧煤电厂都不放过

Source PDF: all report pdf/Anthropic还在疯狂抢算力：8954亿美元承诺，连旧煤电厂都不放过.pdf
Report source: WallstreetCN (Global)
Date: 2026-11-03
Pages: 6
Status: ok

### Page 1

为了满⾜ Claude 模型训练和企业客⼾调⽤需求， Anthropic 不仅与亚⻢逊、⾕歌、微软等云
服务商签订⻓期协议，还开始直接采购芯⽚、参与数据中⼼建设，并向前加密货币矿企和
新型云服务商寻找电⼒与机房资源。
摘要：
AI 公司正在变成⼤型基础设施买家。
Anthropic 的扩张路径，已经从训练模型、争夺企业客⼾，延伸到建设数据中⼼、采购芯⽚和协
调电⼒资源。为了获得⾜够的计算能⼒，这家公司开始直接介⼊过去主要由云服务商和基础设
施运营商承担的⼯作。
美国当地时间 11 ⽉ 2 ⽇， Anthropic 向美国证券交易委员会提交更新后的招股书。⽂件补充了
2026 年第三季度相关财务数据，并披露⻓期采购和租赁安排。
⽂件显⽰，算⼒成为 Anthropic 扩张过程中最重要的成本项。为了满⾜ Claude 模型训练和企业客
⼾调⽤需求，该公司不仅与亚⻢逊、⾕歌、微软等云服务商签订⻓期协议，还开始直接采购芯
⽚、参与数据中⼼建设，并向前加密货币矿企和新型云服务商寻找电⼒与机房资源。

收⼊增⻓ 12 倍，重新出现经营亏损
更新后的招股书显⽰， Anthropic 第三季度营收为 169.6 亿美元，⾼于第⼆季度的 115.6 亿美元，
也远⾼于 2025 年同期的约 13.8 亿美元。
⾸⻚ 资讯 快讯 ⾏情 ⽇历 APP VIP 会员 ⼤师课 ⻅闻历 搜索

### Page 2

今年前三个季度， Anthropic 累计营收 332.42 亿美元，去年同期为 25.45 亿美元，增⻓约 12 倍。
不过，营收增⻓并没有带来经营利润的同步改善。
第三季度， Anthropic 经营费⽤升⾄ 175 亿美元，第⼆季度为 106 亿美元。当季公司录得 5.67 亿美
元经营亏损，⽽第⼆季度曾实现 9.74 亿美元经营利润。计算及基础设施费⽤达到约 99 亿美元，
较第⼆季度的约 56 亿美元增加近⼀倍。
Anthropic 将经营利润转负主要归因于短期算⼒采购。由于客⼾需求增⻓超过此前锁定的算⼒供
应， Anthropic 不得不在市场上临时购买额外计算资源，⽽短期采购价格明显⾼于多年期协议。
第三季度， Anthropic 短期算⼒采购⾦额约 50 亿美元。此外，⼀项与员⼯捐赠配捐计划有关的⾮
现⾦费⽤约 20 亿美元，也对当季经营结果造成了影响。
剔除股权薪酬和上述捐赠配捐费⽤后， Anthropic 第三季度调整后经营利润率约为 15% ，低于第
⼆季度的 19% 。调整后利润率下降，反映出收⼊扩张过程中，算⼒采购和其他经营成本仍在侵

蚀利润空间。
价格端也出现变化。 Anthropic 披露，第⼆季度⾄第三季度，客⼾使⽤量增⻓带来的收⼊增加，
部分被新模型版本降价和合同折扣抵消，影响⾦额约 6 亿美元。
Anthropic 第三季度净亏损为 232.9 亿美元，低于第⼆季度的 663 亿美元。前三个季度累计净亏损
达到 1040.39 亿美元，但其中约 943 亿美元与可转换票据、衍⽣负债等⾦融⼯具公允价值变动有
关，主要受到公司估值上升影响。

### Page 3

8954 亿美元承诺，近⼀半将在 2030 年前兑现
招股书披露，截⾄ 9 ⽉底， Anthropic 通过采购承诺和租赁协议形成的累计⽀出承诺达到 8954 亿
美元，绝⼤部分与计算资源和基础设施有关。
其中，约 4475 亿美元预计在 2030 年 12 ⽉ 31 ⽇前兑现，其余部分延伸⾄ 2031 年⾄ 2045 年。未来
12 个⽉内到期的承诺约 698 亿美元。
这笔⾦额并不等于已经形成的有息债务，也不代表 Anthropic 需要⽴即⽀付全部款项。它包含不
同类型的采购合同、设备租赁和数据中⼼相关协议，付款时间分布在未来多年。但部分合同具
有较强约束⼒，即使实际使⽤量低于预期，公司仍可能需要承担相应付款义务。
从合同构成看，尚未开始的租赁承诺约 3214 亿美元，固定最低采购承诺约 2656 亿美元。这些⻓
期安排将未来的算⼒采购和基础设施使⽤成本提前锁定，也让公司承担了较⻓时间的付款责
任。
截⾄ 9 ⽉底， Anthropic 持有现⾦、现⾦等价物和短期投资合计 1318.45 亿美元。如果计⼊可⽤信

贷额度，公司总流动性约 1466 亿美元。

### Page 4

与账⾯资⾦相⽐，⻓期承诺的规模明显更⼤。 8954 亿美元约为 Anthropic 现⾦及短期投资的 6.8
倍；未来 12 个⽉约 698 亿美元的到期承诺，相当于现有现⾦及短期投资的约 53% 。
招股书也明确提⽰了其中的经营⻛险。 Anthropic 收⼊主要来⾃按使⽤量计费的消费型业务，⼤
部分收⼊没有⻓期合同保障，算⼒和基础设施⽀出却通常需要提前多年锁定。公司可能按照预
期收⼊签署⻓期协议，但未来实际收⼊未必达到预期。
如果客⼾收⼊增⻓⽆法覆盖相关付款，或者公司⽆法以可接受的条件继续融资， Anthropic 可能
⽆法履⾏部分合同。某些商业协议违约还可能触发信贷协议和可转换票据中的交叉违约条款，
导致债务提前到期，甚⾄使供应商终⽌合作。
成本增⻓已经体现在财务数据中。前三个季度， Anthropic 计算及基础设施费⽤同⽐增⻓ 329% ，
增加约 144 亿美元。第三季度相关费⽤约 99 亿美元，占当季营收约 58% 。
从云服务商到矿企， Anthropic 全球寻找算⼒

据外媒报道，去年年底， Anthropic 管理层已经意识到算⼒需求预测存在较⼤不确定性，随后密
集签署了数⼗份算⼒和基础设施协议，合作对象从⼤型科技公司延伸到数据中⼼初创企业、芯
⽚⼚商，以及曾经从事加密货币挖矿的企业。
这轮采购不再局限于购买云服务。 Anthropic 开始招聘数据中⼼建设和供应链专家，直接购买服
务器设备和冷却系统，帮助合作⽅加快机房建设。它还与多个州的数据中⼼开发商接触，研究
电⽹接⼊、项⽬许可，以及将旧煤电⼚和⼯业设施改造为数据中⼼的可能性。
新型云服务商和前加密货币矿企由此进⼊ Anthropic 的供应体系。外媒整合公开资料显⽰，
Fluidstack 、 Lambda 等公司提供算⼒资源， TeraWulf 、 Hut 8 等企业则参与数据中⼼建设。
这些合作⽅掌握着⼟地、电⼒接⼊资源或现成的数据中⼼设施。对 Anthropic ⽽⾔，利⽤已有场
地和电⼒资源，可以减少从零建设⼤型园区所需的时间。对部分前矿企来说，转向 AI 数据中⼼也
提供了新的业务增⻓空间。

但现成场地并不等于可以⽴即交付算⼒。电⼒供应、设备安装和施⼯⼈员都可能拖慢项⽬进
度。
Anthropic 最初寻找的数据中⼼项⽬规模⾄少在 200MW 左右，后来开始接受规模约 50MW 的项
⽬，再通过电池储能和天然⽓发电等⽅式补充供电。公司也考虑把多个⼩型项⽬组合起来，逐
步增加算⼒容量。
Anthropic 也开始直接参与基础设施项⽬的设计和采购。公司购买芯⽚、冷却系统等设备，再交
由合作运营商安装，以缩短从项⽬建设到算⼒上线的时间。
这套模式扩⼤了 Anthropic 可以接触的资源范围，但也增加了项⽬管理的复杂度。不同数据中⼼
的建设周期、设备规格、电⼒条件和交付时间并不⼀致。即使合同已经签署，实际算⼒仍要经
过建设、设备安装、并⽹和调试才能投⼊使⽤。

### Page 5

能源投资机构 Energize Capital 管理合伙⼈约翰 · 塔夫（ John Tough ）认为， Anthropic 并不掌握
完整的算⼒供应管线，只能在全球范围内逐个寻找电⼒和基础设施资源，再把不同规模的项⽬
拼接起来。
Anthropic 在招股书中将这部分专⽤基础设施称为 Anthropic Direct 。公司希望通过⻓期锁定专⽤
容量，减少对价格更⾼的短期算⼒采购的依赖。
外媒援引知情⼈⼠的话透露， Anthropic 预计 2026 年可拥有超过 3.5GW 的算⼒容量，并计划在
2027 年将规模扩⼤⼀倍。
Anthropic 的⻓期算⼒合同，集中在少数⼤型科技公司和基础设施供应商⼿中。
根据招股书及相关披露， Anthropic 与⾕歌的合同⾦额约 1213 亿美元，与亚⻢逊约 1100 亿美元，
与微软约 449 亿美元；与 SpaceX 的相关协议⾦额最⾼约 1205 亿美元，期限延伸⾄ 2036 年。此
外，该公司还与 Fluidstack 和 Lambda 签有相关协议。

博通相关设备租赁承诺约 1612 亿美元，也是此次算⼒⽀出结构中不能忽略的⼀部分。
此外， Anthropic 还计划额外采购约 1250 亿⾄ 1500 亿美元的⾕歌芯⽚，并预计在 AMD 芯⽚上的
⽀出超过 200 亿美元。这些安排没有固定的合同⾦额，因此不计⼊前述 8954 亿美元承诺总额。
多芯⽚架构是 Anthropic 降低供应⻛险的另⼀项安排。该公司在招股书中称，其算⼒资源来⾃
AWS Trainium 、⾕歌 TPU 、英伟达 GPU 和 AMD GPU ，并将不同来源的算⼒统⼀调度，根据不
同 任 务 对 价 格 和 性 能 的 要 求 分 配资 源 。 这 有 助 于 降 低 对 单 ⼀ 芯 ⽚ 供 应 商 的 依赖 ， 但 也 让
Anthropic 与供应链伙伴之间的关系更加复杂。

Document 333: Cliff Notes_ the source and persistence of inflation (2026-11-04)

### Page 1

#cliffnotes #rba #australia #inflation #us #fomc #ecb #aud #usd
Cliff Notes: the source and persistence of inflation
By Ryan Wells, Illiana Jain & Elliot Clarke
11:16 October 30 2026 Share
Key insights from the week that was.
In Australia this week, all eyes were on the September CPI. Headline inflation rose 0.8% (4.3%yr) in
the month, largely owing to a 9.4% jump in fuel prices and a seasonal surge in domestic and
international holiday travel & accommodation. The underlying inflation pulse also proved hotter than

anticipated for Q3 overall, up 1.0% (3.7%yr).

### Page 2

While some parts of the basket are seeing disinflation – housing in particular looks to be cooling faster
than expected as builders lure sales with discounts – other components are still showing persistence.
Highlighting this is market services ex volatiles, which the RBA watches closely as an indicator of price
pressures related to domestic capacity. In Q3, this metric rose 1.2% (3.2%yr), and the monthly detail
points to momentum holding up – in line with our expectations for another chunky read in Q4.

With inflation running well above the RBA’s August forecasts, and some of the energy risks flagged
then materialising, we continue to expect the Monetary Policy Board will deliver a 25bp rate hike to
4.85% in November. Beyond November, another rate hike in February is a risk, but is not currently part
of our base case.
Across the Tasman, the RBNZ this week surprised market expectations for a 25bp rate hike, deciding
instead to wait for further information before taking the next step with policy. The decision was made

4:3, with the Governor in the ‘on hold’ camp. A 25bp hike in December to 3.00% and further tightening
in 2027 (Westpac NZ forecasts a peak of 4.00% in September) seems probable; however, each
decision will depend on fuel prices, inflation expectations and evidence of second-round pricing
pressures, along with the evolution of global economic risks.
In the US, the FOMC also held rates steady at its October meeting. Based on prior comments by key
members, the decision was not a surprise, and neither were Governor Cook and Cleveland Fed

President Hammack’s dissents in favour of a 25bp hike. The Committee remains positive on economic
activity, characterising it in the statement as “expanding at a strong pace” (an upgrade from “solid” in
September). Chair Warsh also subsequently emphasised the breadth of economic growth outside of
tech-related investment (evinced by Q3 GDP the day after; see below).
The Committee refrained from spotlighting key price risks in the statement. However, inflation “remains

elevated” and the FOMC are determined to achieve a “timely” and sustainable return of annual inflation
to 2.0%yr after 6 years above target. This guidance and the “accumulation of more information will help
the Committee determine the policy stance” suggests another 25bp hike is likely in December.
Thereafter, the FOMC will remain attentive to any broadening in inflation and related risks through
2027; although, in the absence of another shock, we expect them to remain on hold.

The Q3 GDP report certainly supported Chair Warsh’s view on the breadth of growth, the 3.4%
annualised gain for GDP benefitting from a 3.8% annualised increase in household consumption,
12.7% growth in non-residential investment as well as a robust increase in government spending.
Notably though, the contribution from business investment was effectively offset by net exports’ drag
given most of the componentry related to AI infrastructure investment is imported. This is not to say that

US firms won’t benefit from the investment once complete, but simply that much of the initial return
goes to the foreign businesses who provide the necessary capital equipment.
On consumption, the monthly PCE data implies downside risks remain for the consumer, the increase
in nominal spending seen in the month requiring dissaving and, on a real basis, consumption flat in the
month following a 0.5% gain in August. Cost-of-living pressures and uncertainty over the outlook is

### Page 3

Related articles
expected to see household consumption growth average around trend over the coming year, albeit with
considerable volatility quarter to quarter. This should see PCE inflation slow back towards its 2.0%yr
medium-term target as the FOMC desires, from 3.6%yr and 3.1%yr for the headline and core
measures respectively as of September.
Across the Atlantic, the ECB also left its key policy rates unchanged in October. Of note from the
meeting communications, energy prices continue to pose upside risks for inflation, but also downside

risks for growth. "While the full effects of the energy shock are still unfolding, underlying inflation has
remained broadly stable so far and wages do not show a material response". "Financing conditions
have [also] tightened", "which is likely to weigh on inflation and growth". In the press conference,
President Lagarde noted that, between the October and December meetings, a significant amount of
new economic information will be released, allowing forecasts and risks assessments to be tested.

Based on the October meeting communications, we continue to expect a rate hike at the December
meeting but believe this will be prove to be the last hike of the cycle. Note though, the spectrum of risks
Europe faces is broad and skewed towards inflation in the near term, hence the Council’s guidance will
remain hawkish. President Lagarde also announced today that she will step down as ECB President in
February ahead of the scheduled conclusion of her term next October.

Document 420: Quantum dictionary, part 1_ Decoding the fundamentals From superposition to decoherence, we unpack the concepts and technologies shaping the future of quantum computing. 2 min read

Source PDF: all report pdf/Quantum dictionary, part 1_ Decoding the fundamentals From superposition to decoherence, we unpack the concepts and technologies shaping the future of quantum computing. 2 min read.pdf
Report source: Bank of America
Date: 2026-10-01
Pages: 9
Status: ok

### Page 1

13030420     1

Transformation 
Quantum dictionary, part 1: Decoding the fundamentals 
21 October 2026

Key takeaways 
• What is quantum computing? How does it work? What is a qubit? And when will quantum reach its full potential? Quantum 
computing challenges our intuition and our understanding of what computers can do. In this two-part dictionary series, we 
decode key terms behind what could be the next major technological revolution. 
• Qubits are the basic building blocks of quantum computers, harnessing properties such as superposition and entanglement to 
process information. However, qubits are highly sensitive to their surroundings where disturbances can lead to errors in 
quantum computation, making error correction a key challenge for building quantum computers. 
• There is no single approach to building a quantum computer. Researchers and companies are pursuing a range of qubit 
technologies, from neutral atoms and photons to spin, superconducting, topological and trapped-ion qubits, while quantum 
computing itself is likely to increasingly work alongside classical computing and AI.

First, let’s lay the groundwork… 
Quantum computing  
Quantum computing uses qubits and quantum properties such as superposition (defined later on) to perform complex 
calculations and solve certain problems faster than classical computers.1 From a practical perspective, quantum computing could 
be used to tackle problems related to optimization, machine learning, simulation and factorization across a range of sectors, 
including autos and logistics, financial services, energy and climate, health and life sciences and national security. 
Current quantum computers can perform ‘just’ thousands of quantum operations. As the technology advances from thousands to 
millions, billions and eventually trillions of operations, quantum computers could begin to have commercial applications.  
• Millions: Financial services and security could experience some of the first use cases, such as threat detection, fraud 
prevention, derivatives pricing, bond trading and arbitrage. 
• Billions: Quantum could help power AI through advanced data generation capabilities.  
• Trillions: Quantum chemistry applications could emerge, including drug discovery, battery simulation and materials 
discovery. 
These are the publicly known applications, according to Oxford Quantum Circuits (OQC). And as quantum computing capabilities 
improve, some of these use cases could emerge sooner than anticipated.  
Now, we’ll tackle the core concepts… 
Qubits 
Qubits (quantum bits) are a quantum computer’s basic unit of information. They can exhibit quantum properties such as 
superposition and entanglement (which we elaborate on below) — properties fundamental to quantum computing. 
Quantum gates manipulate the state of qubits, much like logic gates manipulate bits in classical computers. Despite the name, a 
quantum gate is not a physical object, but rather an operation applied to a quantum system that changes its state. 
From physical to logical qubits 
Physical qubits are the foundation of a quantum computer’s hardware. However, they are highly susceptible to error. To address 
this, multiple physical qubits can be structured together to encode a logical qubit, allowing errors to be detected and corrected

1 Schneider, J., & Smalley, I. (2025, June 10). What is quantum computing? IBM. 
I NSTI TUTE
Accessible version

### Page 2

2 21 October 2026 
  I NSTI TUTE

and making the stored quantum information more reliable. In recent years, according to BofA Global Research, logical qubits 
have become a greater focus for quantum computing companies. 
Entanglement 
Entanglement is a condition where two or more qubits become linked so that their states are correlated, regardless of distance 
(Exhibit 1). It allows quantum computers to process information in ways that can be difficult or impossible for classical 
computers. 
So, how does it work? Suppose we have two photons that have opposite polarizations (the orientation of their light waves). 
Before measurement, neither has a definite polarization. When one is measured as horizontally polarized, the other will be 
vertically polarized when measured in the same basis, wherever it is. When measured separately, they appear to be random, but 
when compared, they are correlated (Exhibit 2). 
The challenges 
Once created, entanglement must be protected from decoherence (where interactions with the environment destroy quantum 
correlations). Maintaining entanglement over long distances is another challenge. Photons traveling through optical fiber can be 
absorbed or scattered, making long-distance quantum communication difficult. 2 Quantum repeaters aim to overcome this 
challenge by extending entanglement across a series of shorter links. 
Exhibit 1: Entanglement is a state where at least two qubits become 
correlated 
An infographic of entangled qubits

Source: BofA Global Research 
BANK OF AMERICA INSTITUTE

Exhibit 2: Measuring one qubit in an entangled pair instantly 
determines the state of the other, no matter the distance 
An example of what entanglement means for qubit measurement

Source: BofA Global Research 
BANK OF AMERICA INSTITUTE

Superposition 
Unlike a classical bit, which can be one of two states (0 or 1), superposition allows a quantum system to exist in multiple states 
or configurations at the same time (Exhibit 3). For example, a qubit can exist in the 0 state, the 1 state or any state that is a 
combination of 0 and 1.3  
A quantum system can maintain a superposition until it is observed or measured. Once measured, its superposition collapses into 
a single state. Another way of thinking about superposition is to imagine a sphere with the north pole representing zero and the 
south pole representing one. Other points on the surface of the sphere represent different superposition states. This allows us 
to: 1) represent complex forms of information; and 2) simulate atoms in a computer. 
Schrödinger’s cat 
In 1935, physicist Erwin Schrödinger proposed a thought experiment to illustrate the strange implications of quantum 
mechanics. In this scenario, a cat, a radioactive substance that decays very slowly, and a Geiger counter are put together in a 
sealed box. According to quantum mechanics, the system can be described as a superposition in which the cat is both alive and 
dead until the box is opened. Once the box is opened and the system is observed, the superposition collapses into a single 
outcome.

2 Rehman, M. (2026, May 11). What is Quantum Entanglement? Quantum Insider. 
3 Microsoft. (n.d.). Superposition.

### Page 3

21 October 2026    3

I NSTI TUTE

Enabling parallel computation versus classical computing 
Superposition is a key attribute of quantum computing that allows for quantum parallelism, or the ability to perform multiple 
computations at the same time. Classical bits can only be in one of two states, 0 or 1. In contrast, a qubit can be in a 
superposition of 0 and 1, allowing a quantum computer to process multiple possible qubit states at once.4  
Exhibit 3: Superposition is the condition where a qubit can exist in a superposition of 0 and 1 
Illustrating the power of superposition

Source: BofA Global Research 
Note: A Bloch sphere is a geometric representation of qubit states. 
BANK OF AMERICA INSTITUTE 
Error rates and fidelity 
Fidelity is a measure of how closely a quantum state or operation matches what was intended (Exhibit 4). A higher fidelity 
generally means fewer errors. 
Physical qubits are prone to errors. One way to address this is to combine multiple physical qubits to create a more reliable 
logical qubit. Quantum error correction (QEC) allows errors in the physical qubits to be detected and corrected, helping protect 
the information stored in the logical qubit.

4 Ibid.

### Page 4

4 21 October 2026 
  I NSTI TUTE

Exhibit 4: Fidelity measures how accurately a qubit retains its intended quantum state during computation 
Infographic showing the implications of high versus low fidelity

Source: BofA Global Research  
BANK OF AMERICA INSTITUTE 
How does quantum error correction work? 
At its simplest, QEC relies on redundancy, or storing information across multiple qubits so that errors can be detected and 
corrected.  
Consider an example: suppose someone wants to send a message represented by 0. Instead of sending a single 0, the message 
could be repeated three times as 000, known as a codeword. If one bit were accidentally flipped from 0 to 1 during transmission, 
the recipient might receive 010. Because two of the three bits are still 0, the recipient could infer that the intended message 
was 000. 
However, if two bits flipped, producing 110 or 011, the recipient could infer the wrong message. If all three flipped to 111, the 
recipient would have no way to know from the received codeword that errors had occurred.5 This approach is known as a 
repetition code and illustrates how redundancy can help protect information from errors.  
Repetition codes are a simple form of error correction that can address bit-flip errors, where a 0 changes to a 1 or vice versa. 
More sophisticated QEC codes are needed to address other quantum errors, such as phase flips.6 Shor’s code, developed by 
American theoretical computer scientist Peter Shor, uses nine physical qubits to encode one logical qubit and can correct one 
bit-flip error or one phase flip error, but it can’t correct both types of errors at the same time. Meanwhile, Steane’s seven-qubit 
code, developed by Oxford physicist Andrew Steane, can also correct both types of errors. It has the advantage of being fault-
tolerant, meaning the error-correction process itself does not introduce additional errors.7 
At what error level might we start seeing quantum advantage? 
Some classical computing systems today operate with extremely high reliability, often described as having "17 nines" of fidelity 
(99.99999999999999999%). By comparison, quantum computers currently achieve fidelity rates of around 99.9%. Gerald 
Mullally, CEO of OQC, has argued that quantum systems would need to add roughly three more nines of fidelity to achieve 
commercial relevance, or quantum advantage. 
Noise and decoherence 
Noise refers to unwanted disturbances that affect quantum systems and can lead to errors in computations (Exhibit 5). It can 
come from sources such as interactions with the environment, electromagnetic interference and temperature fluctuations. 
Coherence is a quantum system’s ability to maintain its quantum properties, including superposition and entanglement. When 
coherence is lost through interactions with the environment, the process is known as “decoherence,” causing the system to 
behave more classically (Exhibit 6).8

5 Microsoft Ignite. (n.d.) Introduction to quantum error correction. 
6 Ibid. 
7 Ibid. 
8 Quandela. (n.d.) Quantum Decoherence.

### Page 5

21 October 2026    5

I NSTI TUTE

To fully control qubits for calculations and information transfer, they need to be carefully protected from their surroundings. 
External factors like noise, energy, changes in Earth’s magnetic field, radiation from Wi-Fi or mobile phones or cosmic rays can 
interfere with the process. Even small disturbances can lead to decoherence, making it difficult to develop large-scale, fault-
tolerant quantum computers.9 
According to QuEra Computing, quantum noise can be mitigated through physical isolation of qubits, the development of more 
precise control techniques and quantum error correction, which helps detect and correct errors in physical qubits.  
Exhibit 5: Noise refers to unwanted disturbances, e.g., interactions 
with the environment, that lead to errors in quantum computation 
An infographic explaining quantum noise

Source: BofA Global Research 
BANK OF AMERICA INSTITUTE

Exhibit 6: Decoherence is when qubits lose their key quantum 
properties, superposition and entanglement 
An infographic of decoherence

Source: BofA Global Research 
BANK OF AMERICA INSTITUTE

Quantum processing unit (QPU) 
Referred to as the brain of the quantum computer, the QPU is the core hardware where qubits reside and where quantum 
computations take place. QPUs rely on quantum behaviors like superposition and entanglement to tackle problems in areas such 
as cryptography, simulation and optimization. Just as a CPU (central processing unit) in classical computing contains multiple 
components, a QPU includes quantum gates (operations that manipulate qubits in a controlled way, similar to logic gates in 
classical computing) and interconnects, which enable communication between qubits and other quantum processors.10 
Next, we’ll look at broader computing architecture… 
Heterogeneous computing  
Heterogeneous computing refers to a future computing landscape likely combining classical and quantum computing, potentially 
unlocking more powerful, faster and cheaper compute and helping solve problems that classical computers cannot. According to 
BofA Global Research, AI and quantum computing could also converge and complement each other, enabling more 
transformative use cases. 
Quantum modality  
A quantum modality refers to the type of physical technology used to create and manipulate qubits. We discuss the modalities in 
greater depth in the following section. 
Lastly, we outline qubit technologies… 
Neutral atom qubit 
A quantum modality that uses individual atoms with no electrical charge as qubits (Exhibit 7). The atoms are held in place using 
focused lasers known as optical tweezers. Neutral atom qubit systems do not require atoms to be ionized, which means fewer 
energy constraints and potentially simpler cooling requirements. Atoms such as rubidium or cesium are typically used. The qubits 
are manipulated by changing the energy levels or positions of the trapped atoms using laser pulses.

9 QuEra. (n.d.) Quantum Noise. 
10 SpinQ. (2025, January 16). What does a Quantum Computer Look Like? Explain Key Parts.

### Page 6

  I NSTI TUTE

Exhibit 7: A quantum modality where atoms with a net electrical charge of zero are the foundation for qubits 
Key features of neutral atoms

Source: BofA Global Research 
BANK OF AMERICA INSTITUTE 
Photonic qubit 
A quantum modality that uses particles of light, known as photons, to carry quantum information (Exhibit 8).11 Optical 
components such as beam splitters, which divide light along different paths, and phase shifters, which change the phase of light, 
can be used to perform quantum operations. Photonic quantum computers can use integrated photonic chips to manipulate 
photons and enable quantum logic. 
Exhibit 8: A quantum modality relying on the quantum properties of light 
Key features of photonic qubits

Source: BofA Global Research 
BANK OF AMERICA INSTITUTE

11 Moore, J. (2025, March 28). The 6 different types of quantum computing technology. TechTarget.

### Page 7

21 October 2026    7

I NSTI TUTE

Spin qubit 
A quantum modality that uses the intrinsic spin of electrons or nuclei in solid-state materials, such as silicon or diamond. These 
spins can be manipulated using magnetic or electric fields, and their states are read using optical or charge-sensing techniques. 
Spin qubits are particularly appealing because they can leverage existing CMOS (complementary metal-oxide semiconductor; a 
technology to build processors) manufacturing infrastructure, making it easier to integrate them with conventional processors. 
Superconducting qubit 
A quantum modality in which electronic circuits create qubits. They are built using Josephson junctions, where two 
superconductors are separated by a thin insulating layer. Microwave pulses manipulate the qubits’ quantum states to run 
computations.12  
Specifically, a resistance-free current oscillates back and forth around a quantum loop. An injected microwave signal excites the 
current into superposition states.13 To enable this process, superconducting materials are cooled to near absolute zero (around 
10-20 millikelvin) using dilution refrigerators, helping reduce thermal noise and maintain coherence. 
Topological qubit 
A quantum modality that aims to encode quantum information using topological properties rather than relying only on individual 
particle states (Exhibit 9). One way to think about this is through the paths certain quasiparticles take as they move around one 
another, creating “braids” in space-time. Because the information can depend on the overall pattern of these braids rather than 
small details of their paths, the approach is designed to be more resilient to certain disturbances. 
Exhibit 9: Resilient to small perturbations but requires special quasiparticles not yet been conclusively demonstrated in nature 
An infographic of topological qubits

Source: BofA Global Research 
BANK OF AMERICA INSTITUTE 
Trapped-ion qubit  
A quantum modality in which ions (electrically charged atoms) are held in place using electromagnetic fields. These ions act as 
the qubits and are controlled by the system with tightly focused laser beams.14 The qubits are held inside ultra-high vacuum 
(UHV) chambers.

### Page 1

Asia Trends

2026 年 11 月 4 日

1/6 
RBA は２回連続の利上げ決定も、景気減速懸念で追加利上げには慎重姿勢 
～インフレ圧力の根強さが利上げを促すも、景気下振れ懸念や追加利上げ期待の後退が豪ドルの重しに～ 
 経済調査部 主席エコノミスト  西濵 徹 
（℡：050-5474-7495）

（要旨） 
○ オーストラリア準備銀行（ＲＢＡ）は、11 月２～３日に開催した定例会合において、政策金
利を 25bp 引き上げて 4.85％とすることを決定した。利上げは２会合連続、年明け以降５回
目となり、政策金利は18 年ぶりの高水準となった。 
○ 背景には、原油高に加え、国内の需給ひっ迫を反映してインフレ圧力が根強く、物価上昇が
幅広い財・サービスに波及していることがある。ＲＢＡは物価見通しを大幅に上方修正し、
インフレ率が目標レンジに収束する時期も従来の想定から後ずれすると見込んでいる。 
○ 一方で、今回の利上げ決定においては、理事９人のうち３人が据え置きを支持するなど、景
気減速への警戒感も強まっている。ブロック総裁は、これまでの利上げによる累積的な影響
を見極める必要性を強調しており、ＲＢＡは当面、政策金利を現行水準に据え置き、物価や
雇用、不動産市場の動向を慎重に見極める展開が予想される。ただし、インフレ圧力が高止
まりすれば追加利上げに動く可能性も残ると考えられる。 
○ 豪ドル相場については、利上げが事前に織り込まれていたことに加え、追加利上げ期待の後
退を受けて弱含んだ。先行きについても、国内景気の減速懸念や米国の金融政策、 中国景気、
中東情勢などの影響を受けつつ、当面は上値の重い展開が続く可能性がある。

### Page 2

2/6 
【RBAは２会合連続の利上げ決定、年明け以降５回目、政策金利は18 年ぶりの高水準に】 
オーストラリア準備銀行（ＲＢＡ）は、11 月２～３日の日程で開催した定例の金融政策委員会に
おいて、政策金利であるオフィシャルキ
ャッシュレート（ＯＣＲ）を 25bp 引き上
げて 4.85％とすることを決定した （図
１）。ＲＢＡは、 ２月の定例会合で２年３
ヶ月ぶりの利上げに動き、５月の定例会
合まで３会合連続の利上げを実施した。
その後、６月会合、８月会合では金利を
据え置く様子見姿勢に転じたものの、９
月会合で３会合ぶりの利上げに動いた。
このため、今回は２会合連続、年明け以
降５回目となる。今回の利上げにより、
ＯＣＲは18 年ぶりの水準となった。 
事前の市場予想では、ＲＢＡが利上げに動くとの見方が大宗を占めた。背景には、ＲＢＡ高官か
ら先行きの政策運営について、タカ派姿勢を維持する発言が相次いだことがある。 なかでもハウザ
ー副総裁は、原油高などを理由にインフレ見通しが「著しく悪化している」として、金融政策では
供給ショックに対処できないとしつつ、 コスト上昇がインフレ期待の上昇に波及することを防ぐ必
要があるとの考えを示した。そのうえ、物価動向がＲＢＡの想定を上回る可能性が高いとの見方を
示すなど、 インフレに対する警戒感を強調した。 同氏の発言は政策運営の方向性を明示したもので
はないものの、インフレ見通しの悪化に加え、インフレ期待を抑え込む必要性を強調したことは、
金融市場に追加利上げを意識させるに十分であったと考えられる（注１）。

注１ 10 月21 日付レポート「弱含む動きをみせてきた豪ドル相場は持ち直しに向かうか」 
  （https://www.dlri.co.jp/report/macro/666763.html）

3/6 
７-９月のインフレ率は前年同期比＋3.9％と前期 （同＋3.9％） から横ばいで推移し、 ＲＢＡが定
める目標レンジ（２～３％）を引き続き上回るとともに、ＲＢＡが８月会合で示した見通し（前年
比＋3.6％）をも上回った（図２） 。前期
比も＋1.3％と前期（同＋0.6％）から上
昇ペースが加速し、 食料品やエネルギー
など生活必需品の物価上昇に加え、サー
ビス物価も上昇基調を強める 動きが確
認された。コアインフレ率（トリム平均
値）も前年同期比＋3.7％と前期（同＋
3.6％）からわずかに伸びが加速してお
り、 ＲＢＡの見通し （同＋3.3％） も上回
っ た。前期 比も＋ 1.0％ と前期（ 同＋
0.8％） から上昇ペースが加速しており、
基調的なインフレ圧力が強まっていることがうかがえた。さらに、ＲＢＡのブロック総裁が議会証
言において、 これまでの利上げにより物価が目標に回帰させることが可能か、今回会合で議論する
考えを示すとともに、物価上昇圧力が依然強いとの認識を示した。このため、金融市場では利上げ
が実施されるとの見方が一段と強まった（注２）。

【評決は６対３に割れる、インフレ見通しを上方修正する一方、景気の下振れリスクにも警戒】 
会合後に公表された声明文では、 理事９人のうち６人が利上げに賛成した一方、 ３人は据え置き
を支持するなど、 金融引き締めの必要性を巡って判断が分かれたことが明らかにされた。 前回の９
月会合では、全会一致で利上げを決定したことを踏まえれば、今回の票割れは、インフレ抑制を優
先する姿勢が維持される一方、 景気減速への警戒が理事会内で強まりつつあることを示唆している。 
物価動向について「依然高水準にある」 、 「足元のデータはインフレの広がりを示唆している」と
の認識を示した。 加えて、 「インフレ見通しも大幅に上方修正された」 として、 その理由に 「イラン
情勢の悪化による燃料やその他商品市況の上昇を一部反映した」ことを挙げた。 一方で、 「同国内に
おける供給能力のひっ迫も想定以上にやや厳しい状況にある」 との見方を示し、「ＡＩ （人工知能）
関連投資が技術関連のインフレ圧力を招いているほか、 多くの財・サービスでインフレ圧力が依然
高い」 、 「多くの企業がコスト上昇圧力に直面するなか、財・サービス価格をすでに引き上げ、ない
し検討を行っており、インフレ率は 2027 年を通じて高止まりすると予想している」とした。その
結果、 「インフレ率が目標レンジに達するのは2027 年後半と従来の想定から約３四半期遅れる」と
して、 「インフレ期待への上振れリスクをもたらしている」との見方を示した。 
会合にあわせて公表した最新の『四半期金融政策報告』では、物価見通しが大幅に上方修正され

### Page 4

4/6 
た。2026 年末のインフレ率は前年同期比＋4.5％と、 ８月会合時点の見通し （同＋3.6％） から大幅
に上方修正された。コアインフレ率についても、2027 年半ばの見通しを前年同期比＋3.5％と、８
月会合時点 （同＋3.0％） から上方修正した。 そのうえで、 ＲＢＡはインフレ率が目標レンジの上限
である３％以下となる時期を 2027 年末近くまで後ずれすると見込んでおり、目標レンジの中央値
である 2.5％への収束も2028 年半ばになるとの見方を示している。 加えて、 原油高などに伴う企業
のコスト増加が販売価格や賃金設定に波及し、 家計や企業のインフレ期待が高止まりするリスクに
も警戒感を示した。 
一方、金融環境については「年明け以降の利上げにより引き締まった」との見方を示した。その
うえで、 「経済は減速し、不動産価格も大幅に下落し、失業率も上昇している 」として、先行きは
「向こう１年で生産の伸びは一段と鈍化が見込まれ、 生産能力への圧力が緩和され、 インフレ率を
目標レンジに戻す一助になることが期待される」 とした。 また、 「金利上昇や不動産価格・取引高の
下落は、 不動産投資や家計消費を下押しする」 とする一方、 「データセンター関連投資を含む、 企業
部門による設備投資は当面堅調な推移が見込まれ」 、 「雇用も増加が見込まれるが、 失業率は８月会
合時点の想定より大幅に上昇する」との見通しを示した。 
ただし、 実体経済や物価見通しについては 「依然として不確実性が高い」 として、 「イラン情勢の
行方は見通せず、インフレ率が想定を上回り、実体経済が想定を下回るシナリオも考えられる」と
し、「不確実性が長期化すれば、 世界経済や同国の経済成長率が低下する可能性がある」 とした。 一
方で、 「足元ではＡＩ関連投資による押し上げ効果がイラン情勢の悪影響を上回っており、主要貿
易相手国の景気は想定を上回る力強さが続いている 」ものの、 「同国では生産性の伸びの鈍化が潜
在成長率の重しとなり、 不動産市場の低迷の度合いや実体経済への影響にも不確実性が残る」 との
見方を示した。 
今回の決定については 「インフレ期待が高止まりするリスクが高まっている」 ほか、 「インフレ見
通しや上振れリスクを踏まえ、 目標レンジに戻すためにはさらなる引き締めが妥当と判断した」と
した。そのうえで、先行きも「インフレ率を持続的に目標レンジに戻すために必要な措置を引き続
き講じる」として、 「経済指標や見通し、リスクに関する評価を注視する」としつつ、 「政策対応は
情勢変化に対応できる態勢が整っており、 物価安定と完全雇用の実現というマンデートに注力する」
との考えを示した。ただし、９月会合では追加利上げに言及する文言が含まれていたものの、今回
はそうした文言は削除されるなど明確な方向感を示されなかった。

【ブロック総裁は過去の利上げ効果を見極める姿勢を強調、追加利上げには慎重な構えをみせる】 
一方で、 今回の会合後に実施されたブロック総裁の記者会見では、 先行きの金融政策運営につい
て慎重な姿勢がうかがわれた。同氏は、今回の利上げ決定について、原油価格の上昇による直接的
なインフレへの対応ではなく、 従来からの国内における需給ひっ迫や、 エネルギー価格の上昇が幅
広い物価に波及する二次的な影響を抑制することへの対応と説明した。 ただし、 年明け以降に実施
した５回の利上げのうち、 直近の利上げの効果は実体経済に十分に浸透していないとして、 今後公
表される経済指標を見極める必要性を強調した。 今回の会合で据え置きを支持した理事についても、

### Page 5

5/6 
不動産市場の調整や失業率の上昇、 賃金上昇率が加速していないことなどを重視し、 金融引き締め
の効果を確認するために時間を置くべきだと判断したことを明らかにした。 
事実、 足元の国内景気にはこれまでの金融引き締めの影響が広がりをみせている。 ９月の失業率
は 4.7％と上昇傾向にあるとともに、 不動産価格の下落や住宅ローン需要が減少していることなど、
金利上昇に敏感な分野で調整圧力が強まる動きが確認されている。 ＲＢＡは今回公表した最新の経
済見通しにおいて、2027 年末の実質GDP 成長率を前年同期比＋1.3％と、 ８月会合時点の見通し （同
＋1.6％）から下方修正している。さらに、失業率も2028 年末に 5.1％まで上昇すると予想してお
り、 ８月会合時点の見通し （4.8％） から上方修正している。 同氏は、 中心的な見通しでは景気後退
を想定していないとしつつ、経済成長率の大幅な鈍化や家計消費の弱さが続くとの認識を示した。 
注目されるのは、 前述した通りＲＢＡが声明文から、 必要に応じて追加利上げを行うとの明示的
な表現を削除したことである。 同氏は、 これを金融政策の方向転換を示すものと受け止めるべきで
はないと説明しており、追加利上げの可能性を排除してはいない。ただし、理事会内において判断
が分かれたことに加え、 これまでの利上げによる累積的な影響を考慮すれば、 ＲＢＡが今後も断続
的に利上げを実施する可能性は低下したと考えられる。 当面は現行水準で政策金利を据え置き、 物
価や雇用、不動産市場の動向を慎重に見極める展開が予想される。一方で、原油価格の高止まりや
インフレ期待の上昇が続けば、2027 年前半に追加利上げに動く可能性も残る。 反対に、 不動産市場
の調整が深刻化するとともに、 個人消費や雇用の悪化が想定以上に進む場合には、 金融引き締めの
長期化を見直す必要性が強まるとみられる。

【豪ドル相場は利上げ決定後に弱含む動き、先行きも当面は上値の重い展開が続く可能性】 
金融市場では、前述の通り今回の利上げが事前に相当程度織り込まれていたこともあり、決定直
後の豪ドル相場は対米ドルで下落した
（図３）。 声明において、追加利上げを明
示する文言が削除されたことに加え、理
事会内 部において 据え置きを支持する
意見がみられたことも、追加的な金融引
き締めの 実施に対する市場の見方を後
退させたと考えられる。 今後の豪ドル相
場を巡っては、ＲＢＡの政策金利が高水
準で維持されることが一定の下支え要
因になると見込まれるものの、 国内景気
の減速に伴って追加利上げ期待が後退
すれば、豪ドル高圧力は限定的となる可能性がある。 
さらに、豪ドル相場は国内の金融政策だけでなく、米国の金融政策や中国景気、国際商品市況、
世界的な投資家のリスク選好の影響を受けやすい。 仮にＲＢＡが追加利上げに踏み切る一方、 米国
の金融引き締め姿勢が弱まれば、 米国とオーストラリアの間の金利差の変化が豪ドル相場を押し上

経済研究所が信ずるに足ると判断した情報に基づき作成していますが、その正確性、完全性に対する責任は負いません。見通しは予告
なく変更されることがあります。また、記載された内容は、第一生命保険ないしはその関連会社の投資方針と常に整合的であるとは限
りません。
6/6
げる可能性がある。しかし、米国でもインフレ圧力が根強く、金融引き締めの長期化観測が強まる
場合には、米ドル高が豪ドルの上値を抑えるとみられる。また、中国景気の減速や中東情勢の悪化
によるリスク回避姿勢の強まりも、豪ドルの下押し要因となり得る。こうした点を踏まえると、豪
ドル相場は短期的に一進一退の展開となるとともに、 明確な上昇基調への転換には、 豪州の景気減
速への懸念後退や米国との金融政策の方向性の違いが一段と明確になることが必要であろう。
以 上

Document 439: The Good the Bad and the Ugly for Rates in 2027

Source PDF: all report pdf/The Good the Bad and the Ugly for Rates in 2027.pdf
Report source: ING Think
Date: 2026-11-04
Pages: 3
Status: ok

### Page 1

The spaghetti-western title neatly captures the three big forces
shaping long-end rates: the ‘good’ of AI-driven productivity, the
‘bad’ of elevated ﬁscal deﬁcits, and the ‘ugly’ of renewed
inﬂationary pressure from ongoing wars. By the middle of 2027,
however, things should start to look a little less ugly as inﬂation
falls

Padhraic Garvey, CFA, Michiel Tukker and Benjamin Schroeder
The good, the bad and the ugly: AI, ﬁscal deﬁcits and inﬂation take centre
stage in the 2027 rates outlook
Key calls and themes and 2027
US rates and a triple whammy of obstacles to overcome
In the US, our baseline view centres on the good, the bad and
the ugly causing trouble in the coming months, and right
through the ﬁrst quarter of 2027 at least, to prompt a return of
the 10yr US Treasury yield to the 5.25%-plus area (vs 4.9%
currently). We’d cap the absolute upside at 5.5%, as waiting
later in 2027 is a 2%-plus handle for GDP growth and inﬂation,
alongside a stable but not worsening debt dynamic narrative.

### Page 2

The fall in inﬂation here is key, as that allows the Federal
Reserve to step away and for the market to re-discount cuts.
That allows long-dated yields to edge back down, with the
10yr targeting 4.75% for starters and likely settling in the 4.5-
4.75% zone, which is still above normal, but rightfully so. We
don't call for it, but a decision to materially cut long-end
Treasury funding would push in the same direction.
Euro rates have good reason to be nervous

For euro rates, we also expect a bumpy ride at the start of
2027, whereby markets anxiously weigh each inﬂation reading
for signs of second-round eﬀects. If all goes well, we should see
a signiﬁcant repricing of the European Central Bank path,
helping the 2yr EUR swap rate down by some 50bp. The 10yr
rate will be stickier, however, as a global rise in real rates adds
upward pressure to longer euro rates. If inﬂation does not ease
as hoped, then we could see a stretch higher in rates from here.

In such a scenario, the 10yr swap rate could push well above
4%.
European bond spreads in a duel with the "bad"
Fiscal strains and election risk are putting eurozone bonds to
the test. France is the key fault line, while Bunds’ safe-haven
appeal masks mounting supply pressures. We see a turbulent
ﬁrst half of 2027, and any relief hinges on a credible path to
French ﬁscal consolidation
A delayed landing for GBP rates
Sterling rates are well above levels we consider fair value, but

trauma from turning too dovish too quickly is still on investors'
minds. Only once the Bank of England can prove its ability to
bring inﬂation closer to target (by the end of 2027) do we
pencil in a signiﬁcant repricing lower for GBP rates. Relatively
high sensitivity to US rates means we don't expect the path
lower to be a smooth one. And with gilt markets increasingly
reliant on risk-sensitive investors, we think a material term risk
premium remains justiﬁed.

The grand Dutch pension reforms ﬁnale
Dutch pension reforms are reaching their ﬁnale as almost

€900bn of assets are scheduled to transition in January 2027.
The reforms should keep the 10s30s euro swap curve
structurally steeper, but macro will continue to be the main
driver of the curve for now. Under the new system, we expect
more swap ﬂows throughout the year as the need for interest
rate hedges moves in tandem with asset prices (i.e. equities).

CENTRAL BANK MEETINGS

People Bank of China (October 20)

We expect the PBOC to keep the 1Y LPR 
unchanged next week . Industrial 
production is expected to edge up from 
5.2% yoy in August to 5.3% in September, 
supported by firm external demand. 
Exports stayed strong, rising 36.3% yoy 
in September, driven by the regional AI -
electronics upcycle.

Domestic momentum, however, remains 
uneven. Retail sales growth is expected 
to moderate to 0.3% yoy  in September. 
Household sentiment is weak amid 
uncertain job prospects, slower income 
growth and elevated precautionary 
savings, while subdued property prices 
continue to weigh on household wealth 
and consumption. Investment also stays 
weak, with fixed as set investment 
expected to contract further by 7.5% yoy 
ytd in September. Corporate investment 
is cautious amid the anti -involution 
campaign and geopolitical tensions, 
while the property sector continues to be 
a major drag.

Policymakers are likely to maintain a 
measured policy stance, relying on 
targeted fiscal support to sustain growth 
rather than further rate cuts.

Bank Indonesia and Bangko Sentral ng 
Pilipinas (Oct 21 and 22).

Bank Indonesia is expected to extend its 
pause, as they lean on non-rate measures, 
including intervention and hedging 
facilities to help the currency.  On Friday, 
MSCI signaled a  potential shift in 
Indonesia’s status to a standalone 
status, rather than a reclassification to 
frontier market , after consultations are 
complete in mid -2027. Any ensuing risk -
off move in the currency , would likely 
prompt greater central bank vigilance 
and intervention.

Our base case for the benchmark rate to 
be held unchanged this quarter , backed 
by a lower conviction around back -to-
back US rate hikes in Oct and relative 
rupiah stability. Separately, the 
government announced additional 
support for low-income households in 4Q 
by way of in -kind assistance and wage 
subsidies. This year’s fiscal deficit i s 
likely to close in at -2.85% of GDP, close 
to our forecast at -2.9%, but few 
uncertainties facing the 2027 math (note).

We expect a 25bp hike from the BSP to 
5.25% in October , after three rate 
increases earlier in the year. Rising by the 
highest pace in Asia, Philippines inflation 
surged to 7.2% y/y in September from 6.1% 
in August, matching this year's peak and 
approaching a three -year high.  Inflation 
risks remain tilted to the upside, driven by 
further public transport fare adjustments, 
elevated oil prices, recent wage hikes 
and peso depreciation. Fiscal space is, 
meanwhile, limited to support 
households, via subsidies and/ instate 
price caps. Governor Remolona also 
signalled that the BSP remains ready to 
act on rates to contain inflation. With 
inflationary pressures likely to be 
persistent, the likelihood of further hikes 
in 4Q26 rises.

### Page 3

The Week Ahead October 16, 2026 
                                                                                                                                         3

Bank of Korea (October 22)

The Bank of Korea is expected to hold its 
policy rate at 3.00%, following back -to-
back rate hikes in July and August. AI -
driven growth momentum remains strong, 
as reflected in September export growth 
of around 80% YoY. However, the 
urgency for further tightening has eased, 
with CPI inflation stabilising at 2.9% YoY 
in September. The recent KOSPI 
correction and continued KRW strength 
also reduce the need for another near -
term hike. We maintain our call for a final 
25bp rate hike in November, bringing the 
policy rate to 3.25% by year-end.

FORTHCOMING DATA 
RELEASES

Japan

September trade and CPI data are due 
shortly. Export growth is expected to 
remain solid at 24% YoY, supported by 
continued strength in AI -related 
semiconductor exports. CPI inflation is 
projected to rise notably to 2.6% YoY, 
driven by the phase -out of publ ic utility 
subsidies, higher global energy prices, 
and persistent inflationary pressures 
from a weak yen. The BOJ is likely to 
increasingly acknowledge that its 2% 
inflation target is within reach. However, 
it is expected to keep rates unchanged 
at 1.25% at its October 29–30 meeting to 
assess the impact of the latest rate hike 
in September.

Taiwan

September export orders and industrial 
production data are due shortly. Export 
orders are expected to sustain 60% YoY 
growth, while industrial production is 
projected to expand by 20% YoY. The 
global AI boom faces growing headwinds 
from hyperscalers’ cash flow pressures, 
central bank rate hikes, and higher bond 
yields. However, our high -frequency 
indicators across AI demand (token 
expenditure), investment (hyperscaler  
capex and data centre construction 
spending), and supply (semiconductor 
sales and exports) show no clear signs of 
a significant slowdown so far.

Malaysia

Malaysia’s external demand likely 
remained strong in September 2026, 
while inflation edged up slightly. We 
expect export growth to sustain its 
robust expansion at 45% yoy , with 
momentum anchored by strong demand 
for electrical and electronics goods. Oil 
& gas shipments likely remained buoyant. 
However, high base effects prevented a 
faster pace of expansion.

We expect headline inflation to rise 
modestly to 2.1% yoy in September 2026. 
The renewed increase in global oil prices 
likely contributed to higher increases in 
domestic energy costs, although fuel 
subsidies continued to limit the cost 
pass-through and keep underlying 
inflationary pressures contained. Food 
inflation likely also firmed, with adverse 
weather conditions associated with El 
Nino posing upside risks.

### Page 4

The Week Ahead October 16, 2026 
                                                                                                                                         4

Singapore

We expect Singapore’s core and 
headline inflation to edge up slightly to 
2.3% yoy and 2.4% yoy , respectively, in 
September 2026, following modest 
increases to 2.2% yoy and 2.3% yoy in 
August. Inflationary pressures likely 
reflected firm private transport price 
increases amid higher petrol prices 
despite slower car price growth , the 
ongoing pass -through of higher global 
energy prices to fuel -exposed services 
items, positive contribution from 
elevated electricity & gas prices, firmer 
food inflation, and a slight increase in 
still-muted accommodation inflation. 
Firm, albeit not acc elerating, inflation in 
September 2026, which may be close to 
its peak in the Monetary Authority of 
Singapore’s assessment, likely validated 
its decision to maintain SGD NEER policy 
settings at the October 2026 review, 
following two proactive tightening 
moves in April and July.

Hong Kong

CPI is projected to edge up from 1.7% yoy 
in August to 1.9% in September, 
supported by improving consumption 
sentiment and higher global energy 
prices. Mainland visitor arrivals 
increased by 29.0% yoy in September on 
a daily average basis, providing furthe r 
support to retail spending and 
consumer-related services. Meanwhile, 
renewed conflict in the Middle East 
during the month has pushed up global 
energy prices, adding to upward 
pressure on utility and transport costs.

Document 463: US Economy Continues To Roar, While The Fed Waits For More

Source PDF: all report pdf/US Economy Continues To Roar, While The Fed Waits For More.pdf
Report source: researchmail轉寄
Date: 2026-10-30
Pages: 9
Status: ok

### Page 1

From:    Yardeni QuickTakes <yardeni-research@ghost.io>
Subject: US Economy Continues To Roar, While The Fed Waits For More
Date:    4 hours ago
US Economy Continues To Roar, While The Fed Waits For More

YARDENI QUICKTAKES
US Economy Continues To Roar,
While The Fed Waits For More
By Ed Yardeni & Elias Griepentrog • 29 Oct 2026
View in browser
Photo by Pawan Sharma / Unsplash

### Page 2

The FOMC voted 10-2 on Wednesday to hold the federal funds rate at
3.75%-4.00%, with the two dissenters favoring a 25bps hike. Yet Fed
Chair Kevin Warsh's assessment of the US economy sounded decidedly
hawkish. Economic growth is robust and broadening, the labor market is
in good shape, inflation remains above the Fed's 2.0% y/y target, and
financial conditions are hardly tight. So why did the Fed stand pat?
Warsh argued that September's hike was already tightening financial

conditions, inflation expectations remained anchored, and stronger
productivity growth was allowing the economy to expand without
overheating. With an "information-rich" period ahead, the Fed saw little
reason to rush, he said.
In our view, the decision was puzzling. It reminds us of someone standing
in a room with a fire alarm blaring and smoke pouring under the door, yet
insisting on more evidence before calling the fire department. Thursday's
economic data only reinforced our conviction that the Fed should have

raised rates by 25bps on Wednesday. Let's take a closer look.
(1) GDP. The US economy expanded at a 3.4% annualized rate in Q3-
2026, the largest gain in a year (chart).
A closer look reveals remarkably strong underlying demand. Real final
sales to private domestic purchasers, excluding volatile trade and
inventory swings, surged 4.9% (saar), the best increase since Q2-2021
(chart)! Consumer spending advanced 3.8% for the second consecutive
quarter, its fastest pace since Q4-2024. Nonresidential fixed investment

### Page 3

soared 12.7%, a pace last exceeded outside the pandemic period in Q2-
1978. The AI investment boom was a major driver, with investment in
information-processing equipment surging 37.4% and software investment
rising 9.6%. Yet headline GDP understated this strength because net
exports subtracted 3.32 percentage points from growth. Imports surged
19.7%, led by AI-related durable goods. Exports fell 5.7%.
Consumer spending and business investment are driving the US

economic boom. Federal government spending is too, rising 15.8% last
quarter.
The Weekly Economic Index rose to 3.3% for the week ended October 16,
signaling a solid start to Q4's real GDP growth (chart).
(2) Inflation. In Q3's GDP report, core PCED inflation remained
troublesome at 3.0% y/y, well above the Fed's 2.0% target (chart). On the

### Page 4

brighter side, it rose at an annualized rate of 2.4% during the quarter.
Headline PCED inflation rose 0.5% m/m in September, lifting its yearly
rate to 3.6% (chart). Core PCED inflation, excluding food and energy,
increased 0.3% m/m and 3.1% y/y. Both remain stubbornly above the
Fed's inflation target. Furthermore, Warsh's inflation diffusion measure
shows that nearly half of PCED components rose more than 3.0% y/y,
compared with a pre-pandemic average of just 32%.

Goods PCED inflation rose to 3.9% y/y in September from 3.6% in August,
partly reflecting a 7.3% m/m increase in gasoline prices (chart).

### Page 5

Tariff-related price pressures have yet to subside, while the AI investment
boom is adding to inflation across the technology sector. Prices for
computers and software are rising at record annual rates (chart). Together,
these forces suggest goods inflation is unlikely to ease meaningfully soon.
Services PCED inflation rose to 3.5% y/y in September (chart). Supercore
services inflation, excluding housing and energy, edged up to 3.6% y/y
(chart). It has been trending higher since late 2025, underscoring

persistent underlying price pressures.

### Page 6

Services inflation remains broad-based, with most major categories rising
around 3.0% y/y or more. Transportation services inflation has been
particularly troublesome as higher energy costs feed through to service
prices (chart).
(3) Consumer spending and income. Americans continue to shop. Real
consumer spending remained at a record high in September, even as real
disposable personal income (DPI) declined for the first time since April
(chart). Real DPI has been essentially flat for over a year, a trend likely to

persist as more Baby Boomers retire.

### Page 7

September's saving rate declined to 3.8%, the lowest since 2022 (chart).
We expect it to fall further as retiring Baby Boomers continue spending
their sizeable net worth because they are no longer earning labor income.
Real consumer spending was flat in September, as a 0.3% m/m decline in
goods purchases offset a 0.2% increase in services spending. Spending
on restaurants and hotels remained particularly robust, rising 0.5%
following a 1.1% surge in August (chart).

### Page 8

(4) Labor market. Initial unemployment claims fell to just 194,000 during
the week of October 24, marking the tenth reading below 200,000 this
year, the most in any year since 1969! The four-week moving average
also fell to its lowest level since October 1969. Continuing claims also
declined, approaching their lowest level since April 2023. These figures
underscore the labor market's remarkable resilience (chart).
💡

leave comments or questions at the end of our QuickTakes, and it
may be featured in the next "Just Ask Dr. Ed!"

### Page 9

clause.

Document 549: スペインは解散総選挙へ

Source PDF: all report pdf/スペインは解散総選挙へ.pdf
Report source: MURC
Date: 2026-10-07
Pages: 4
Status: ok

### Page 1

2026 年 10 月 7 日

MURC Focus

スペインは解散総選挙へ

～右派連立政権が成立する見込み～

調査部 主任研究員 土田 陽介

1. 解散総選挙に臨んだサンチェス首相 
スペインのペドロ・サンチェス首相が 10 月 5 日、議会を解散し、11 月 29 日に総選挙（定数 350、比例代表制）
を行うと発表した。住宅価格や家賃の高騰に対する政府の緊急政令案が、10 月 2 日に下院で否決されたことを
受けて、少数与党による政権運営の行き詰まりを意識した首相が、国民に信を問う決断をした形である 。ただし、
サンチェス首相が率いる中道左派の与党・社会労働党（PSOE）の支持率は低迷している（図表 1）。

もともと PSOE の人気は低迷しており、前回 2023 年 7 月の総選挙でも、ライバル政党である中道右派の国民
党（PP）が第一党となっていた。しかしサンチェス首相は他の左派政党や地域政党の協力を受けて組閣し、政権
を維持した経緯がある。しかし首相周辺の汚職疑惑などから PSOE の人気は回復せず、次期の総選挙では PP
と同根の右派政党である Vox による連立政権が成立する公算が大きい。 
図表 1. 主要政党の支持率調査 図表 2. スペインの住宅価格

（出所）各種世論調査より
